## 5. TAG CENSUS

### WPC (CIK 0001025378, FYE December (FYE 1231; all three most recent 10-Ks period-end 2025-12-31, 2024-12-31, 2023-12-31))

- `(NO TAG EXISTS)` — FFO / AFFO — THE CENTRAL QUESTION · n/a · FY0-0 · none · FFO is NOT tagged at all, by any route. Verified three independent ways this session: (1) companyfacts CIK0001025378.json contains 731 tags across dei/srt/us-gaap/ffd — zero match /FundsFrom|FFO|AFFO/ (the only hits were false positives: StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueRestrictedStockAwardNetOfForfeitures). (2) The FY2025 10-K taxonomy wpc-20251231.xsd declares 757 company extension elements — zero contain FundsFromOperations, FFO, AFFO, SameStore, SameProperty, MaintenanceCapital, RecurringCapital or TenantImprovement. (3) FilingSummary.xml for the FY2025 10-K lists no FFO report section. Scanning the raw instance wpc-20251231_htm.xml for element names matching /Funds/ returns exactly one: wpc:FundsCapitalizedForConstructionInProgress (unrelated). Conclusion: it is not undimensioned, not dimension-locked, not an extension — it is simply absent from the 10-K XBRL. WPC's FFO/AFFO lives only in the earnings release / supplemental, which is not XBRL-tagged.
- `us-gaap:NetIncomeLoss` — Net income (parent) · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=708,334,000; FY2024=460,839,000; FY2025=466,359,000 · Continuous FY2008-FY2025, no gaps on annual 10-K frames. Equals us-gaap:NetIncomeLossAvailableToCommonStockholdersBasic in all three years (no preferred outstanding).
- `us-gaap:ProfitLoss` — Net income including noncontrolling interests · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=708,264,000; FY2024=460,630,000; FY2025=472,915,000 · Diverges from NetIncomeLoss by the NCI share (FY2025 gap 6,556,000). An FFO build must pick one deliberately; NAREIT FFO is attributable to common.
- `us-gaap:DepreciationAndAmortization` — D&A — INCOME STATEMENT expense line · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=574,212,000; FY2024=487,724,000; FY2025=521,127,000 · Placement verified empirically: this element id appears in R5.htm (CONSOLIDATED STATEMENTS OF INCOME) of the FY2025 10-K. It is a TOTAL D&A — there is NO real-estate-specific depreciation expense tag for WPC. It bundles building depreciation with lease-intangible amortisation.
- `us-gaap:DepreciationAmortizationAndAccretionNet` — D&A — CASH FLOW add-back (different number) · USD · FY2011-2025 · FY2023=594,166,000; FY2024=507,101,000; FY2025=537,645,000 · Placement verified: appears in R9.htm (CONSOLIDATED STATEMENTS OF CASH FLOWS). Runs 16,518,000 ABOVE the income-statement D&A in FY2025 (19,954,000 in FY2023, 19,377,000 in FY2024). Both are undimensioned annual facts in companyfacts, so a naive 'find the D&A tag' extractor picks arbitrarily and the FFO answer moves by ~$16-20m.
- `us-gaap:AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets` — Intangible amortisation (subset of D&A) · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023=247,500,000; FY2024=186,700,000; FY2025=174,700,000 · Note the rounding: values end in 00,000 (decimals=-5), i.e. reported to the nearest $100k, while the income statement is to the nearest $1k. Subtracting this from DepreciationAndAmortization to isolate building depreciation mixes precisions.
- `us-gaap:Depreciation` — Depreciation alone — RETIRED TAG · USD · FY2011-2019 · FY2017=143,900,000; FY2018=162,600,000; FY2019=229,000,000 · Tag-generation change: WPC stopped reporting a standalone Depreciation figure after FY2019. Any series built on it dead-ends at 2019 with no warning.
- `us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfProperties` — Gain/loss on sale of real estate — income statement · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=315,984,000; FY2024=74,822,000; FY2025=193,793,000 · Element id confirmed present in BOTH R5.htm (income statement) and R9.htm (cash flow reversal) of the FY2025 10-K. Continuous FY2008-FY2025. This is the gain a NAREIT-style FFO removes.
- `us-gaap:SaleAndLeasebackTransactionGainLossNet` — Sale-leaseback gain — a SECOND gain line · USD · FY2022-2025 · FY2023=14,715,000; FY2024=22,754,000; FY2025=56,419,000 · Undimensioned annual fact, but its element id does NOT appear in R5.htm — it lives in a note, not on the face of the income statement. So whether it is already inside GainLossOnSaleOfProperties is NOT determinable from the tags alone. Subtracting both risks double-counting; subtracting neither leaves a growing gain in FFO (56m in FY2025, up 2.5x in two years). This must be resolved by reading the note, not guessed.
- `us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfPropertiesBeforeApplicableIncomeTaxes; us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfPropertiesNetOfApplicableIncomeTaxes; us-gaap:GainsLossesOnSalesOfInvestmentRealEstate` — Gains superseded by the above — RETIRED TAGS · USD · FY2010-2019 · BeforeTaxes FY2017=33,878,000, FY2018=118,605,000, FY2019=18,143,000; NetOfTaxes FY2015=6,487,000, FY2016=71,318,000, FY2017=33,878,000; InvestmentRealEstate FY2013=-332,000, FY2014=1,581,000, FY2015=6,487,000 · Three overlapping gain tags used in different eras, with overlapping years carrying identical values (FY2017 = 33,878,000 under two tags; FY2015 = 6,487,000 under two tags). Summing gain tags across a long history would double-count.
- `us-gaap:ImpairmentOfRealEstate` — Real-estate impairment · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023=86,411,000; FY2024=43,595,000; FY2025=70,367,000 · Element id confirmed in BOTH R5.htm and R9.htm of the FY2025 10-K. Continuous FY2009-FY2025. NAREIT FFO adds impairments back; a net-income-plus-depreciation-minus-gains formula does not, understating FFO by 70m in FY2025.
- `us-gaap:AssetImpairmentCharges; us-gaap:GoodwillImpairmentLoss; us-gaap:OtherAssetImpairmentCharges; us-gaap:ImpairmentOfLongLivedAssetsToBeDisposedOf; us-gaap:DirectFinancingLeaseImpairmentLoss` — Other impairments — RETIRED / SPARSE · USD · FY2008-2024 · AssetImpairmentCharges FY2014=23,067,000, FY2015=29,906,000, FY2016=59,303,000 (ends FY2016); GoodwillImpairmentLoss FY2022=29,334,000, FY2023=0, FY2024=0 (GAPS 2017,2018,2019); DirectFinancingLeaseImpairmentLoss FY2022=-4,700,000 only · Five impairment tags used across eras with gaps and no overlap discipline. DirectFinancingLeaseImpairmentLoss is filed as a NEGATIVE number (-4,700,000) — sign convention differs from the other impairment tags, which are positive.
- `us-gaap:Revenues` — Total revenue · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=1,741,358,000; FY2024=1,583,018,000; FY2025=1,716,485,000 · Continuous FY2008-FY2025, no gaps. The FY2024 dip is real, not a tagging artefact (see spin-off hazard).
- `us-gaap:OperatingLeaseLeaseIncome; us-gaap:OperatingLeaseLeaseIncomeLeasePayments; us-gaap:OperatingLeaseVariableLeaseIncome` — Rental revenue (lease income components) · USD · FY2019-2025 · LeaseIncome FY2023=1,427,376,000, FY2024=1,331,788,000, FY2025=1,479,204,000; LeasePayments FY2025=1,313,926,000; VariableLeaseIncome FY2025=165,278,000 · ASC 842 tags, start FY2019 only. Predecessors: us-gaap:OperatingLeasesIncomeStatementLeaseRevenue (FY2013-FY2014) and us-gaap:RealEstateRevenueNet (FY2013-FY2017, with a GAP at FY2014). Three tag generations for one line; no single rental-revenue series spans 2008-2025.
- `us-gaap:DirectFinancingLeaseLeaseIncome; us-gaap:SalesTypeLeaseLeaseIncome` — Finance-lease income (income that bypasses rental revenue) · USD · FY2024-2025 · DirectFinancing FY2024=34,375,000, FY2025=30,447,000; SalesType FY2024=13,280,000, FY2025=1,502,000 · Only two years undimensioned. A net-lease REIT earns a real slice of its return through sales-type/direct-financing leases whose income never touches the operating-lease line — relevant to the repo's existing ebitdaCoverage guard.
- `us-gaap:StraightLineRentAdjustments` — Straight-line rent adjustment · USD · FY2010-2025 · FY2023=-75,435,000; FY2024=-83,094,000; FY2025=-78,374,000 · TAGGED, and this is the desk's Munger question made numeric. Element id confirmed in R9.htm (cash flow) of the FY2025 10-K — it is the non-cash reversal, filed NEGATIVE (revenue booked ahead of cash). GAPS at FY2013 and FY2014 on annual frames. FY2025: 78.4m of the 1,479.2m operating-lease income was booked but not collected — 5.3% of lease income, and 16.8% of the 466.4m net income.
- `us-gaap:BelowMarketLeaseNet` — Above/below-market lease intangibles — BALANCE ONLY undimensioned · USD · FY2013-2025 · FY2023=136,872,000; FY2024=119,831,000; FY2025=104,055,000 · This is the BALANCE (instant), not the amortisation. There is NO undimensioned amortisation-of-above/below-market-lease income tag. The favorable (above-market) side is an EXTENSION, wpc:FiniteLivedIntangibleAssetOffMarketLeaseFavorableNet, invisible in companyfacts. Inferring amortisation from the year-over-year balance change is contaminated by acquisitions, dispositions and FX.
- `wpc:AmortizationOfRentRelatedIntangiblesAndDeferredRentalRevenue` — Above/below-market + deferred rent amortisation — EXTENSION, cash flow · USD · FY2023-2025 · FY2023=33,958,000; FY2024=24,477,000; FY2025=5,986,000 · Read directly out of wpc-20251231_htm.xml, undimensioned, and confirmed on R9.htm (cash flow). This IS the non-cash lease-intangible amortisation running through revenue — and it is invisible in companyfacts because the namespace is wpc:, not us-gaap:. First/last FY here are the three years carried in the FY2025 10-K, the only filing I parsed at instance level; earlier history was not checked.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireRealEstate` — Capex — acquisitions · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=1,211,397,000; FY2024=1,128,809,000; FY2025=1,657,339,000 · Continuous FY2008-FY2025. This is growth spend, not upkeep.
- `wpc:PaymentsToAcquireRealEstateUnderConstruction` — Capex — development/construction — EXTENSION · USD · FY2023-2025 · FY2023=121,625,000; FY2024=135,327,000; FY2025=191,213,000 · Undimensioned in the instance, on the investing section of R9.htm, and COMPLETELY ABSENT from companyfacts because it is an extension. The us-gaap predecessor us-gaap:PaymentsToDevelopRealEstateAssets ran FY2008-FY2017 (FY2017=14,565,000) and was retired. So via companyfacts alone, WPC's development spend appears to have gone to zero after 2017 when it in fact grew to 191m.
- `us-gaap:RealEstateImprovements` — Capex — building improvements — DIMENSION-LOCKED · USD · FY2023-2025 · Axis MortgageLoansOnRealEstateDescriptionTypeOfPropertyAxis: RealEstateSubjectToOperatingLeaseMember FY2023=54,667,000, FY2024=46,184,000, FY2025=72,703,000; OperatingRealEstateMember FY2023=4,593,000, FY2024=4,702,000, FY2025=5,303,000 (FY2025 sum=78,006,000) · ABSENT from companyfacts (0 hits in the 731-tag list) yet present in the instance — every fact is dimension-locked on a property-type axis with no undimensioned total. This is the nearest thing WPC has to upkeep capex, but it is a Schedule III roll-forward of costs capitalised subsequent to acquisition — an ACCRUAL, not a cash outflow, and not a management-defined 'maintenance capex'. Reaching it requires parsing the instance and summing two members.
- `us-gaap:InterestCostsCapitalized` — Capitalised interest · USD · FY2019-2025 · FY2023=600,000; FY2024=1,000,000; FY2025=1,100,000 · Tagged and undimensioned FY2019-FY2025. Small for WPC (1.1m in FY2025). Predecessors us-gaap:InterestPaidCapitalized (FY2009-FY2011, FY2011=21,168,000) and us-gaap:InterestPaidNet (FY2008-FY2010) are different concepts with a similar name — do not chain them. A separate extension, wpc:FundsCapitalizedForConstructionInProgress, reads 98,200,000 for FY2025 in the instance.
- `us-gaap:LongTermDebt; us-gaap:DebtLongtermAndShorttermCombinedAmount` — Total debt · USD · FY2012-2025 · Both tags: FY2023=8,144,182,000; FY2024=8,039,002,000; FY2025=8,722,690,000 · Two tags carrying IDENTICAL values in every overlapping year (FY2015-FY2025). Redundant, not additive — an extractor that sums debt tags would double WPC's leverage. Components: us-gaap:SeniorNotes FY2025=6,950,261,000; us-gaap:UnsecuredDebt FY2025=1,196,366,000; us-gaap:SecuredDebt FY2025=140,646,000; us-gaap:LineOfCredit FY2025=435,417,000. Those four sum to 8,722,690,000 = the total, so they are the parts.
- `us-gaap:DebtWeightedAverageInterestRate` — Weighted-average interest rate · pure · FY2022-2025 · FY2023=0.046; FY2024=0.046; FY2025=0.05 · TAGGED and undimensioned, FY2022-FY2025 only — four years. Rate rose to 5.0% in FY2025 against a maturity wall (see below).
- `us-gaap:LongTermDebtMaturitiesRepaymentsOfPrincipalInNextTwelveMonths; ...InYearTwo; ...InYearThree; ...InYearFour; ...InYearFive; ...AfterYearFive` — Debt maturity ladder · USD · FY2011-2025 · FY2025 ladder: NTM=979,753,000; Y2=597,920,000; Y3=1,279,593,000; Y4=1,535,867,000; Y5=1,017,519,000; after Y5=3,381,294,000 (sums to 8,791,946,000) · Complete and continuous FY2011-FY2025 across all six rungs. The ladder sums to 8,791,946,000 vs LongTermDebt 8,722,690,000 — a 69,256,000 gap (discounts/issuance costs), so the ladder is face value and the balance sheet is carrying value. Note us-gaap:LongTermDebtMaturitiesRepaymentsOfPrincipalRemainderOfFiscalYear appears in 10-Qs only (37 facts) and never on an annual frame.
- `us-gaap:PreferredStockSharesIssued; us-gaap:PreferredStockValue` — Preferred equity · shares / USD · FY2011-2025 · SharesIssued FY2023=0, FY2024=0, FY2025=0; PreferredStockValue FY2023=0, FY2024=0, FY2025=0 · WPC has NO preferred outstanding. 50,000,000 shares authorised (us-gaap:PreferredStockSharesAuthorized), none issued. This is why NetIncomeLoss equals NetIncomeLossAvailableToCommonStockholdersBasic.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsOfDividendsCommonStock` — Common dividends paid (cash) · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=916,530,000; FY2024=765,146,000; FY2025=790,032,000 · Continuous FY2008-FY2025, confirmed undimensioned in the FY2025 instance. This is the reliable one for WPC. us-gaap:Dividends (FY2010-FY2025, FY2025=799,907,000) is the declared figure and differs by 9,875,000.
- `us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared` — Dividends per share declared · USD/shares · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=4.067; FY2024=3.49; FY2025=3.62 · Continuous FY2008-FY2025. The FY2023-to-FY2024 drop from 4.067 to 3.49 is a genuine reset, not a tagging error (see the spin-off hazard). Note the instance also carries a Q4-only duration fact (2025-10-01 to 2025-12-31) of 0.92 — a period-length filter is mandatory.
- `us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid` — Dividends per share 'cash paid' — NOT THE CASH RATE · USD/shares · FY2012-2025 · FY2023=5.0347; FY2024=3.47; FY2025=3.58 · This tag carries the TAX-CHARACTER total from the REIT distribution table, not the cash dividend rate. Verbatim from the FY2025 10-K text block (wpc:ScheduleOfDistributionsPaidPerShareForTaxTableTextBlock): 'Amount for the year ended December 31, 2023 includes a distribution of $0.7627 per share representing the taxable distribution of shares of NLOP that occurred in conjunction with the Spin-Off on November 1, 2023'. So FY2023's 5.0347 includes 0.7627 of NON-CASH in-kind stock. It decomposes into three extensions in the instance: wpc:CommonStockOrdinaryIncomeDividendsPerShareCashPaid (FY2025=3.3754), wpc:CommonStockCapitalGainsDividendsPerShareCashPaid (FY2025=0.2046), wpc:CommonStockReturnOfCapitalDividendsPerShareCashPaid (FY2025=0, FY2024=0.1628, FY2023=0.8671). The return-of-capital component is the Graham question in the filer's own words: in FY2023, 0.8671 of 5.0347 — 17% of the distribution — was legally a return of capital, not earnings.
- ABSENT: No FFO tag of any kind (us-gaap, srt, or wpc extension) — verified in companyfacts, in the FY2025 taxonomy .xsd, and in FilingSummary.xml.; No AFFO tag of any kind.; No same-store or same-property NOI tag. Zero matches for /SameStore|SameProperty/ in the 731-tag companyfacts list and in the 757 extension elements; no such report section in FilingSummary.xml. It is text/supplemental only, if disclosed at all.; No maintenance or recurring capital expenditure tag. Zero matches for /MaintenanceCapital|RecurringCapital|TenantImprovement/ in either the companyfacts tags or the extension elements.; No real-estate-specific depreciation EXPENSE tag. Only total D&A. (Schedule III carries us-gaap:RealEstateInvestmentPropertyAccumulatedDepreciation, a balance, not an expense.); No undimensioned amortisation-of-above/below-market-lease income tag — only the balance us-gaap:BelowMarketLeaseNet, plus the wpc: extension on the cash flow.; No occupancy, leased-percentage or weighted-average-lease-term tag on an annual undimensioned frame.; No us-gaap:OperatingIncomeLoss after FY2015 (tag ran FY2012-FY2015 only) — an EBITDA proxy built on the operating line dead-ends in 2015 for WPC.
- HAZARDS: FFO IS NOT TAGGED. Any FFO figure for WPC must be computed by the pipeline from primitives; there is no filed number to reconcile against. The desk should say so on the page rather than imply the company reported it.; THE SPIN-OFF SEAM AT FY2023/FY2024. wpc:DistributionsInConnectionWithTheSpinOff = 777,272,000 for FY2023, and the FY2025 10-K names the Spin-Off as occurring 2023-11-01 with one NLOP share per 15 WPC shares at $11.44 per NLOP share. Revenue falls 1,741,358,000 -> 1,583,018,000 and declared dividends per share fall 4.067 -> 3.49 across that seam. Both moves are structural, not operational deterioration. A continuity tripwire that flags 'dividend cut' or 'revenue decline' here would be reporting an artefact of a corporate action.; TWO D&A TAGS, BOTH UNDIMENSIONED, BOTH ANNUAL, DIFFERING BY ~16-20m. DepreciationAndAmortization is the income statement (R5.htm); DepreciationAmortizationAndAccretionNet is the cash flow (R9.htm). Nothing in companyfacts distinguishes them. Pin the tag explicitly.; TWO GAIN TAGS AND THE DOUBLE-COUNT RISK. GainLossOnSaleOfProperties is on the income statement; SaleAndLeasebackTransactionGainLossNet (FY2025=56,419,000) is not on the face of it. Whether the second is inside the first is undecidable from tags. Withhold rather than guess.; THREE HISTORICAL GAIN TAGS WITH IDENTICAL VALUES IN OVERLAP YEARS (FY2015=6,487,000 under two tags; FY2017=33,878,000 under two tags). Summing across generations double-counts.; MAINTENANCE CAPEX IS NOT SEPARABLE. The FY2025 cash flow investing section contains only PaymentsToAcquireRealEstate, wpc:PaymentsToAcquireRealEstateUnderConstruction, loan/JV items and PaymentsForProceedsFromOtherInvestingActivities. There is no recurring-capex, tenant-improvement or leasing-commission line. AFFO cannot be built for WPC from filed tags. The only upkeep proxy, us-gaap:RealEstateImprovements, is dimension-locked and is an accrual roll-forward.; us-gaap:PaymentsForCapitalImprovements EXISTS in companyfacts with 2 facts, both 10-Q, neither on an annual frame (ends 2015-06-30 and 2016-06-30). An extractor that does not filter form=10-K and period length would pick a quarter and call it a year.; CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid IS A TAX FIGURE, NOT A CASH RATE — FY2023's 5.0347 includes 0.7627 of in-kind NLOP shares. Using it as the dividend rate overstates FY2023 by 24% against declared 4.067 and mislabels a stock distribution as cash.; EXTENSION TAGS ARE INVISIBLE IN COMPANYFACTS. Verified: wpc:PaymentsToAcquireRealEstateUnderConstruction (191,213,000 in FY2025) and wpc:AmortizationOfRentRelatedIntangiblesAndDeferredRentalRevenue exist in the instance but appear nowhere in the 731-tag companyfacts list. WPC declares 757 extension elements. A companyfacts-only pipeline sees a materially incomplete cash flow statement.; DIMENSION-LOCKED us-gaap TAGS ARE ALSO INVISIBLE IN COMPANYFACTS. Verified with us-gaap:RealEstateImprovements: present in the instance under two members of MortgageLoansOnRealEstateDescriptionTypeOfPropertyAxis, absent from companyfacts entirely. So 'not in companyfacts' does NOT mean 'not tagged'.; STRAY NON-FISCAL PERIOD ENDS. Some FY-labelled facts carry end dates that are not 12-31 (e.g. FiniteLivedIntangibleAssetAcquiredInPlaceLeases fy=2013 fp=FY with end=2013-11-30, val=872,000 vs the FY2012 value of 447,278,000). Selecting 'the latest fact in the fiscal year' without requiring end to match the fiscal year end produces a 500x error on that tag.; LEGACY LEASE TAXONOMY OVERHANG. WPC's companyfacts carries the entire pre-ASC-842 CapitalLeases* and OperatingLeasesFutureMinimumPayments* families alongside the post-2019 Lessor/Lessee families. Any generic 'lease obligations' roll-up must be era-aware.

### NLY (CIK 0001043219, FYE December (FYE 1231; 10-Ks period-end 2025-12-31, 2024-12-31, 2023-12-31))

- `(NO TAG EXISTS)` — FFO / AFFO — THE CENTRAL QUESTION · n/a · FY0-0 · none · NOT TAGGED, and the frame does not apply. companyfacts CIK0001043219.json holds 584 tags; zero match /FundsFrom|FFO|AFFO/ (the sole hit, MortgageLoansInProcessOfForeclosureAmount, is a false positive on 'ForeClosure'). The FY2025 taxonomy nly-20251231.xsd declares 237 extension elements; zero contain FundsFromOperations, FFO, AFFO, SameStore, SameProperty or Maintenance. There is also no tag for Annaly's own non-GAAP measure — nothing in the taxonomy or FilingSummary.xml matching 'Earnings Available for Distribution' or 'Core Earnings'. Annaly is a leveraged securities portfolio, not a pile of buildings: adding depreciation back to net income is meaningless here.
- `(NO TAG EXISTS)` — NO STRAIGHT-LINE RENT ANYWHERE · n/a · FY0-0 · none · grep of nly-20251231_htm.xml for 'StraightLineRent' returns 0 occurrences. No rental revenue, no lease intangibles, no tenant base. Correct for the business.
- `us-gaap:RealEstateInvestments; us-gaap:RealEstateInvestmentPropertyNet; us-gaap:RealEstateAccumulatedDepreciation; us-gaap:Depreciation; us-gaap:GainsLossesOnSalesOfInvestmentRealEstate` — THE ABANDONED PROPERTY BOOK — a stranded tag family · USD · FY2013-2021 · RealEstateInvestments FY2019=725,638,000, FY2020=656,314,000, FY2021=0; RealEstateInvestmentPropertyNet FY2018=654,638,000, FY2019=616,822,000, FY2020=563,765,000 (ends); us-gaap:Depreciation FY2015=12,661,000, FY2016=21,868,000, FY2017=17,336,000 (ends FY2017); GainsLossesOnSalesOfInvestmentRealEstate FY2018=5,100,000, FY2019=7,500,000, FY2020=19,700,000 (ends) · Annaly ran an actual property/commercial-real-estate book that it wound down: RealEstateInvestments goes to exactly 0 at FY2021 and the whole Schedule III family (RealEstateGrossAtCarryingValue, RealEstateAndAccumulatedDepreciation*, SECScheduleIIIRealEstate*) stops at FY2020. us-gaap:LiabilitiesOfDisposalGroupIncludingDiscontinuedOperation reads FY2020=0, FY2021=154,956,000, FY2022=0 — the disposal shows in the tags. A REIT extractor that keys off 'does this filer have real-estate tags' will classify NLY as a property REIT on 2013-2020 history and then find nothing after.
- `us-gaap:NetIncomeLoss` — Net income (parent) · USD · FY2007-2025 · FY2023=-1,643,171,000; FY2024=1,001,906,000; FY2025=2,027,262,000 · Continuous FY2007-FY2025, 19 years, no gaps. FY2023 was a large loss.
- `us-gaap:NetIncomeLossAvailableToCommonStockholdersBasic` — Net income to common · USD · FY2007-2025 · FY2023=-1,784,847,000; FY2024=847,355,000; FY2025=1,869,331,000 · Continuous FY2007-FY2025. This is the correct numerator against common dividends. us-gaap:ProfitLoss (with NCI) reads FY2025=2,051,690,000.
- `us-gaap:ComprehensiveIncomeNetOfTax` — Comprehensive income · USD · FY2007-2025 · FY2023=730,325,000; FY2024=1,319,624,000; FY2025=2,556,378,000 · For a mark-to-market securities REIT this is closer to economic result than net income: FY2023 net income was -1,643,171,000 while comprehensive income was +730,325,000, a swing of 2.4bn sitting in OCI. Judging NLY's dividend against net income alone in FY2023 gives a badly wrong picture in the other direction.
- `us-gaap:InterestIncomeOperating; us-gaap:InterestExpense; us-gaap:InterestExpenseOperating; us-gaap:InterestIncomeExpenseNet` — Interest income / expense / net · USD · FY2007-2025 · InterestIncomeOperating FY2023=3,731,581,000, FY2024=4,840,034,000, FY2025=5,959,205,000; InterestExpense and InterestExpenseOperating BOTH FY2023=3,842,965,000, FY2024=4,592,238,000, FY2025=4,823,705,000; InterestIncomeExpenseNet FY2023=-111,384,000, FY2024=247,796,000, FY2025=1,135,500,000 · InterestExpense (FY2007-FY2025) and InterestExpenseOperating (FY2022-FY2025) carry IDENTICAL values in all four overlapping years — redundant duplicate tagging, not two components. Summing them doubles NLY's interest cost. Note FY2023 net interest income was NEGATIVE (-111,384,000) while 1,517,762,000 of dividends were paid.
- `us-gaap:SecuritiesSoldUnderAgreementsToRepurchase -> us-gaap:SecuritiesSoldUnderAgreementsToRepurchaseGross` — Repo funding — TAG GENERATION CHANGE AT FY2022 · USD · FY2008-2025 · OLD tag FY2020=64,825,239,000, FY2021=54,769,643,000, FY2022=59,512,597,000 then STOPS; NEW tag SecuritiesSoldUnderAgreementsToRepurchaseGross FY2023=64,469,836,000, FY2024=68,302,011,000, FY2025=84,303,067,000 · THE most dangerous seam in this filer. The classic repo tag ends at FY2022 with exactly ONE overlap year. Confirmed at instance level: SecuritiesSoldUnderAgreementsToRepurchase does not appear undimensioned in the FY2025 10-K, while ...Gross does (84,303,067,000 at 2025-12-31). A third tag, us-gaap:SecuritySoldUnderAgreementToRepurchaseAfterOffsetSubjectToMasterNettingArrangement (FY2022-FY2025), reads FY2025=81,865,723,000 — the net-of-offset figure, 2.4bn lower. Three tags, three numbers, one liability. An extractor on the classic tag reports NLY's borrowings as ending in 2022; one that sums reports ~166bn against 119bn of total liabilities.
- `us-gaap:Liabilities` — Total liabilities (the leverage sanity check) · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023=81,882,145,000; FY2024=90,859,432,000; FY2025=119,449,927,000 · Continuous FY2008-FY2025. Use this to bound any repo figure: FY2025 repo gross of 84.3bn against total liabilities of 119.4bn is coherent; a summed 166bn is not.
- `us-gaap:OtherSecuredFinancings` — Other secured financings · USD · FY2015-2025 · FY2023=500,000,000; FY2024=750,000,000; FY2025=1,075,000,000 · Continuous FY2015-FY2025. us-gaap:LongTermDebt exists only FY2015-FY2020 (with a GAP at FY2018) and stops.
- `us-gaap:DebtWeightedAverageInterestRate` — Weighted-average interest rate · pure · FY2022-2025 · FY2023=0.0809; FY2024=0.0721; FY2025=0.0644 · TAGGED and undimensioned, FY2022-FY2025 only, confirmed in the FY2025 instance at 2025-12-31 = 0.0644. Four years of history.
- `us-gaap:PreferredStockValue; us-gaap:PreferredStockSharesOutstanding; us-gaap:PreferredStockDividendsAndOtherAdjustments` — Preferred equity · USD / shares · FY2007-2025 · PreferredStockValue FY2023=1,536,569,000, FY2024=1,536,569,000, FY2025=1,802,480,000 (GAPS FY2012-FY2016); SharesOutstanding FY2023=63,500,000, FY2024=63,500,000, FY2025=74,500,000 (FY2017-FY2025 only); PreferredStockDividendsAndOtherAdjustments FY2023=141,676,000, FY2024=154,551,000, FY2025=157,931,000 (continuous FY2007-FY2025) · Preferred is real and growing (1.8bn at FY2025, 157.9m of annual preferred dividends ahead of the common). Note us-gaap:DividendsPreferredStockCash exists only FY2008-FY2010 — the current preferred dividend lives under PreferredStockDividendsAndOtherAdjustments, a DIFFERENT tag from the one AGNC uses. Five-year gap FY2012-FY2016 in PreferredStockValue.
- `us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared` — Dividends per share declared · USD/shares · FY2007-2025 · FY2023=2.60; FY2024=2.60; FY2025=2.80 · Continuous FY2007-FY2025, 19 years, no gaps. THIS IS THE ONE TO USE for NLY's annual per-share dividend.
- `us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid` — Dividends per share 'cash paid' — CARRIES THE QUARTERLY RATE IN AN ANNUAL CONTEXT · USD/shares · FY2015-2025 · FY2023=0.65; FY2024=0.65; FY2025=0.70 · WRONG-NUMBER GENERATOR, confirmed at instance level. In nly-20251231_htm.xml the fact with context start=2025-01-01 end=2025-12-31 has value 0.70, while CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared over the identical annual context is 2.80. NLY tags a QUARTERLY per-share rate against a FULL-YEAR duration. Reading this tag as the annual dividend understates NLY by 4x. Worse, companyfacts also carries a degenerate fact with start=end=2024-01-31 valued 0.70 in the FY2025 filing but 0.65 in the FY2024 filing — the same context restated to a different number across filings.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsOfDividendsCommonStock` — Common dividends paid — tag says 'payments', value is the year-end PAYABLE · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023=325,052,000; FY2024=375,932,000; FY2025=494,881,000 · WRONG-NUMBER GENERATOR, confirmed at instance level. These values are IDENTICAL, to the dollar, to us-gaap:DividendsPayableCurrentAndNoncurrent at each year end (FY2023=325,052,000; FY2024=375,932,000; FY2025=494,881,000). Meanwhile us-gaap:PaymentsOfDividends (total, incl. preferred) reads FY2025=1,882,237,000 and preferred dividends are 157,931,000, implying common paid of roughly 1,724,306,000 — about 3.5x the tagged figure. GAPS at FY2011-FY2014 as well. Do not use this tag for NLY.

- ABSENT: No FFO tag. No AFFO tag. Verified in companyfacts (249 tags) and in the FY2025 taxonomy (294 extension elements).; No 'Net Spread and Dollar Roll Income' or other AGNC non-GAAP earnings tag.; No tangible-net-book-value-per-share tag under any namespace in the FY2025 taxonomy.; No depreciation tag of any kind — not us-gaap:Depreciation, not DepreciationAndAmortization, not DepreciationDepletionAndAmortization.; No real-estate, rental-revenue, lease-income, straight-line-rent, above/below-market-lease or in-place-lease tag.; No gain-on-sale-of-property or property-impairment tag.; No same-store or same-property NOI tag.; No capital expenditure tag of any kind, maintenance or otherwise.; No capitalised interest tag.; No us-gaap:Revenues tag — AGNC does not report a revenue line; the income statement is interest income less interest expense.; No debt maturity ladder tags (no LongTermDebtMaturitiesRepaymentsOfPrincipal* family).; No us-gaap:OperatingIncomeLoss tag.
- HAZARDS: THE SAME ELEMENT, THE SAME INSTANT, TWO DIFFERENT UNDIMENSIONED VALUES. us-gaap:SecuritiesSoldUnderAgreementsToRepurchase at 2025-12-31 appears as both 85,286,000,000 and 85,300,000,000 in agnc-20251231_htm.xml (and 60,798,000,000 / 60,800,000,000 at 2024-12-31) — the balance sheet at $1m precision and a narrative 'approximately $85.3 billion'. companyfacts happens to surface 85,286,000,000, but an instance-level extractor with a first-match or last-match rule will silently pick either. Rounding-aware de-duplication is required.; EVERYTHING IS ROUNDED TO $1,000,000 (decimals=-6). AGNC's figures carry six trailing zeros by construction. Presenting them at dollar precision, or differencing two of them to derive a small residual, manufactures false precision.; INTEREST EXPENSE TAG CHANGES AT FY2023 (InterestExpense -> InterestExpenseOperating, identical in the overlap). Old-tag series dies at FY2023; summing double-counts FY2022-FY2023.; WEIGHTED-AVERAGE RATE TAG CHANGES AND THE TWO TAGS DISAGREE (FY2023: 0.056 vs 0.0486). DebtWeightedAverageInterestRate stops at FY2023.; CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid DISAPPEARS AFTER FY2024, and reads 1.56 for FY2023 against 1.44 declared. CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared has a five-year hole (FY2013-FY2017).; SUB-ANNUAL DURATIONS IN THE 10-K. The FY2025 instance carries CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared over 2022-12-01 to 2022-12-31 valued 0.12. Without a 340-400 day period filter this becomes 'AGNC's annual dividend is 12 cents'.; PREFERRED SHARE COUNTS STOP AT FY2016 while preferred VALUE continues to FY2025 — any preferred-per-share metric is stranded, and PreferredStockValue has a gap at FY2017.; AGNC IS ABSENT FROM src/data/reit-subsectors.json entirely, and the file offers no 'mortgage' subsector among its 13 values.; APPLYING THE REIT FRAME AT ALL IS THE REAL HAZARD HERE. AGNC has no buildings, no tenants, no depreciation and no capex. A page that shows 'FFO —' next to AGNC implies the measure exists and was not found; the honest presentation is that the measure does not apply.

> THE DESK'S CENTRAL QUESTION, ANSWERED FOR THIS TIER: FFO is not tagged at all — for any of the three, by any route. This is not 'dimension-locked' and not 'buried in a reconciliation'. It is absent. Verified independently three ways per filer: (a) companyfacts tag census (WPC 731 tags, NLY 584, AGNC 249) — zero /FundsFrom|FFO|AFFO/ matches, only false positives on 'OfForfeitures', 'OfForeclosure' and 'oFFOrward'; (b) the FY2025 10-K company taxonomy .xsd, which lists every extension element the filer defines (WPC 757, NLY 237, AGNC 294) — zero FFO elements; (c) FilingSummary.xml, which lists every rendered report section — no FFO statement. Whatever FFO the market quotes for WPC comes from the earnings release and supplemental, which are not XBRL-tagged. The pipeline must either compute FFO itself and say so, or decline it.

> COMPANYFACTS IS NOT THE FILING. Two distinct blind spots proven empirically this session, both on WPC. (1) EXTENSION NAMESPACE: wpc:PaymentsToAcquireRealEstateUnderConstruction is undimensioned, sits on the face of the FY2025 cash flow statement (R9.htm), reads 191,213,000 for FY2025 — and appears NOWHERE in companyfacts. Same for wpc:AmortizationOfRentRelatedIntangiblesAndDeferredRentalRevenue and agnc:ComprehensiveIncomeAvailableToCommonShareholders. (2) DIMENSION-LOCKED us-gaap TAGS: us-gaap:RealEstateImprovements returns 0 hits in WPC's 731-tag companyfacts list, yet the instance carries it under two members of MortgageLoansOnRealEstateDescriptionTypeOfPropertyAxis (FY2025 = 72,703,000 + 5,303,000). Corollary for the correctness campaign: 'not in companyfacts' must never be reported as 'the company does not disclose it'. Withhold-never-guess cuts both ways — an absence claim is itself a claim.

> THIS TIER IS TWO DIFFERENT BUSINESSES AND SHOULD NOT SHARE A TEMPLATE. WPC is a net-lease property trust with buildings, tenants, straight-line rent and a genuine (if unbuildable) AFFO question. NLY and AGNC are leveraged agency-MBS portfolios: no buildings, no rent, no depreciation, no capex. AGNC has zero real-estate concepts in its entire 249-tag record. NLY once had a property book and wound it down — its real-estate tags run FY2013 to FY2021 and then read exactly 0. The repo's existing routing (groupingColumns.mjs line 330-335: 'FFO is a property-trust measure: adding depreciation back is meaningless for a mortgage REIT') already has the right instinct and returns NA. That judgement is correct and should be kept.

> JUDGEMENT ON THE EXISTING ffo() DEFINITION (src/lib/reits.mjs:14-17, netIncome + depreciation - gainOnSaleRealEstate): ADEQUATE AS A LABELLED APPROXIMATION, INADEQUATE AS FFO. Priced on WPC FY2025 from confirmed tags: 466,359,000 + 521,127,000 - 193,793,000 = 793,693,000. Four identified departures, all measurable. (1) It omits real-estate impairment, which NAREIT adds back: us-gaap:ImpairmentOfRealEstate FY2025 = 70,367,000, i.e. 8.9% understated. (2) It removes only one gain line; us-gaap:SaleAndLeasebackTransactionGainLossNet FY2025 = 56,419,000 is a separate undimensioned annual fact whose relationship to GainLossOnSaleOfProperties is not determinable from tags. (3) The depreciation input is ambiguous — DepreciationAndAmortization (income statement, 521,127,000) vs DepreciationAmortizationAndAccretionNet (cash flow, 537,645,000), a 16,518,000 spread with nothing in companyfacts to distinguish them; I confirmed placement only by fetching R5.htm and R9.htm. (4) NetIncomeLoss vs ProfitLoss differ by the NCI share (6,556,000 in FY2025). The scorecard note already concedes 'It is an approximation here' — that honesty is the right posture, and the note should additionally say that impairments are not added back and that a second gain line exists.

> AFFO CANNOT BE BUILT FOR ANY OF THE THREE, AND THIS IS THE HONEST FINDING RATHER THAN A GAP TO FILL. There is no maintenance capex, recurring capex, tenant improvement or leasing commission tag anywhere — not in us-gaap, not in any of the three extension taxonomies. WPC's FY2025 investing section contains only acquisitions and construction. The nearest upkeep proxy, us-gaap:RealEstateImprovements, is dimension-locked and is a Schedule III accrual roll-forward, not cash. Munger's point about 'maintenance capex defined by management' turns out to be worse than he framed it: management does not define it in the machine-readable record at all.

> SAME-STORE / SAME-PROPERTY NOI IS NOT TAGGED FOR ANY OF THE THREE. Zero matches across all companyfacts tags and all three extension taxonomies; no such report section in any FilingSummary.xml. Text and supplemental only. A desk that wants it must parse prose, and should not present it as a filed figure.

> NO DUAL-CIK / OPERATING-PARTNERSHIP CO-FILER IN THIS TIER. Checked the SGML headers of all three latest 10-Ks: each has exactly one FILER block — W. P. Carey Inc. (0001025378), ANNALY CAPITAL MANAGEMENT INC (0001043219), AGNC Investment Corp. (0001423689). No paired LP. WPC is not an UPREIT with a co-filing partnership; NLY and AGNC are corporations. The paired-entity hazard is real for the equity-REIT tiers but does not bite here. All three are SIC 6798 and all three have FYE December 31, so no fiscal-year alignment problem in this tier.

> THREE INDEPENDENT WRONG-NUMBER GENERATORS FOUND, ALL IN DIVIDENDS, ALL VERIFIED AT INSTANCE LEVEL. (1) NLY us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid = 0.70 over the full-year FY2025 context against 2.80 declared — a quarterly rate in an annual context, 4x understatement, and no period-length heuristic can catch it. (2) NLY us-gaap:PaymentsOfDividendsCommonStock = 494,881,000 for FY2025, identical to the dollar to the year-end DividendsPayable balance, against roughly 1,724,306,000 of actual common dividends paid — ~3.5x understatement. (3) WPC us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid = 5.0347 for FY2023, which the filing's own text block states includes 0.7627 per share of NON-CASH NLOP shares distributed in the Spin-Off — a tax-character figure masquerading as a cash rate. RECOMMENDATION: for the payout test, prefer CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared for per-share, and PaymentsOfDividends (total cash out) cross-checked against DividendsCommonStockCash for the aggregate. Treat CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid and PaymentsOfDividendsCommonStock as untrusted in this tier until per-filer verified.

> A CHEAP, GENERAL TRIPWIRE FALLS OUT OF THIS: assert that per-share dividends declared and per-share dividends paid agree within a tolerance, and that aggregate common dividends paid divided by weighted average shares agrees with the per-share figure. Both NLY defects and the WPC defect trip it. A second tripwire: any tag whose annual value exactly equals a balance-sheet payable at the same year end is suspect.

> TAG-GENERATION CHANGES CLUSTER AT FY2022-FY2023 ACROSS THIS TIER AND WILL RECUR. NLY repo: SecuritiesSoldUnderAgreementsToRepurchase ends FY2022, ...Gross begins FY2022 (one overlap year), plus a third net-of-offset tag. AGNC and NLY interest: InterestExpense -> InterestExpenseOperating with identical values in the overlap. WPC development capex: us-gaap:PaymentsToDevelopRealEstateAssets ends FY2017, replaced by an extension. In every case the old tag simply stops with no marker. A stranded-tag detector should flag any tag that ends before the latest filed fiscal year while the filer is still filing, and should never sum a tag pair that carries identical values in an overlap year.

> REDUNDANT-TAG DOUBLE-COUNT RISK IS PERVASIVE. WPC: LongTermDebt and DebtLongtermAndShorttermCombinedAmount are identical in every overlapping year (FY2015-FY2025). AGNC: DividendsCommonStock and DividendsCommonStockCash are identical FY2023-FY2025. NLY and AGNC: InterestExpense and InterestExpenseOperating identical in overlap. Any 'sum all matching tags' roll-up inflates leverage, dividends or interest cost. The existing debtToAssets under-capture guard in reits.mjs catches the too-LOW direction; nothing currently guards the too-HIGH direction.

> PRECISION IS NOT UNIFORM AND MUST NOT BE PRESENTED AS IF IT WERE. WPC and NLY file to the nearest $1,000; AGNC files to the nearest $1,000,000. Within WPC, AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets is filed to the nearest $100,000 while the income statement it belongs to is to the nearest $1,000 — differencing them to isolate building depreciation produces a residual with false precision.

> reit-subsectors.json (149 entries, 13 subsector values) HAS NO 'mortgage' CATEGORY AND CONTAINS NEITHER AGNC NOR NLY. WPC is correctly 'net-lease'. If mortgage REITs are to appear on the terminal at all, they need their own routing and their own page — net interest income, comprehensive income, repo leverage, book value — not a property scorecard with dashes in the FFO column. This is a gate question for the desk, not something to fill in silently.

> METHOD NOTE ON WHAT I DID NOT VERIFY: the extension-tag findings (wpc:PaymentsToAcquireRealEstateUnderConstruction, wpc:AmortizationOfRentRelatedIntangiblesAndDeferredRentalRevenue, agnc:ComprehensiveIncomeAvailableToCommonShareholders, agnc:RequiredAnnualDistributionOfTaxableNetIncome, us-gaap:RealEstateImprovements dimension members) come from parsing ONE filing per company, the FY2025 10-K instance, which carries three years of income-statement and cash-flow data and two years of balance-sheet data. Their firstFY/lastFY are stated as the years I actually saw, not as full history. Earlier-year availability of those extensions was not checked and should not be assumed. All companyfacts-sourced first/last fiscal years were computed on facts filtered to form='10-K' with period end matching 12-31 and, for durations, a 340-400 day length.

### PLD (CIK 0001045609, FYE 12)

- `us-gaap:NetIncomeLoss` — Net income attributable to parent · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023 3,059,214,000; FY2024 3,731,635,000; FY2025 3,328,231,000 · Continuous, no seam. us-gaap:ProfitLoss (incl. NCI) also continuous 2009-2025: 3,253,145,000 / 3,947,935,000 / 3,565,299,000.
- `us-gaap:DepreciationAndAmortization` — Total depreciation and amortisation (income statement / cash flow add-back) · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023 2,484,891,000; FY2024 2,580,519,000; FY2025 2,626,028,000 · This is TOTAL D&A, not real-estate-only. It includes amortisation of in-place-lease and other intangibles. NAREIT FFO adds back real-estate depreciation only; using this tag overstates the add-back by ~614m in FY2025.
- `us-gaap:SECScheduleIIIRealEstateAccumulatedDepreciationDepreciationExpense` — Real-estate-only depreciation (Schedule III rollforward) · USD · FY2011-2025 · FY2023 1,837,145,000; FY2024 1,925,386,000; FY2025 2,011,907,000 · THE tag for a real-estate-specific add-back. Predecessor us-gaap:RealEstateAccumulatedDepreciationDepreciationExpense runs FY2009-FY2012 only (element deprecated); the two butt-joint at FY2012/FY2013 with no overlap year.

Any concept list that tries this tag first gets a wrong number for FY2009-2015 and null thereafter.
- `us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfProperties` — Gains on property sales — legacy tags only · USD · FY2013-2018 · FY2016 469,900,000; FY2017 667,300,000; FY2018 635,900,000 · DEAD after FY2018. Also us-gaap:DisposalGroupNotDiscontinuedOperationGainLossOnDisposal FY2009-2018 and us-gaap:GainsLossesOnSalesOfInvestmentRealEstate FY2011-2012. From FY2019 forward there is NO undimensioned us-gaap gains-on-sale tag for PLD in companyfacts at all. I swept every tag matching Gain/Dispos/Sale/Impair with an annual 10-K frame ending 2019 or later: only FX, fair-value-hedge and debt-extinguishment tags survive.
- `pld:GainsOnDispositionsOfDevelopmentPropertiesAndLandNet` — Gains on property sales — where they actually live now · USD · FY2023-2025 · FY2023 462,270,000; FY2024 413,743,000; FY2025 257,731,000 · Company EXTENSION element, read verbatim off R4.htm/R8.htm of accession 0001193125-26-051453. Extensions do not appear in the companyfacts API (PLD's namespaces there are only dei/invest/srt/us-gaap). Value-probed companyfacts for 257,731,000 — zero hits.
- `pld:GainsOnOtherDispositionsOfInvestmentsInRealEstateNet` — Gains on other real-estate dispositions · USD · FY2023-2025 · FY2023 161,039,000; FY2024 904,136,000; FY2025 685,831,000 · Extension; invisible to companyfacts. Combined with the line above, FY2025 property gains = 943,562,000, FY2024 = 1,317,879,000, FY2023 = 623,309,000.
- `pld:OperatingIncomeLossBeforeGainsLossOnRealEstateTransactionsNet` — Operating income BEFORE property gains · USD · FY2023-2025 · FY2023 3,084,483,000; FY2024 3,098,041,000; FY2025 3,414,302,000 · Extension. The us-gaap:OperatingIncomeLoss PLD files (FY2025 4,357,864,000) sits BELOW the gains and therefore INCLUDES them. Any margin or EBITDA-coverage built on us-gaap:OperatingIncomeLoss for PLD counts asset-sale gains as operations — precisely the flattery the desk is meant to strip.
- `us-gaap:Revenues` — Total revenue · USD · FY2016-2025 · FY2023 8,023,469,000; FY2024 8,201,610,000; FY2025 8,790,127,000 · Predecessor us-gaap:RealEstateRevenueNet FY2009-FY2017 (element deprecated 2018-01-31); the two overlap FY2016-2017 so the seam is checkable. PLD does NOT tag RevenueFromContractWithCustomerExcludingAssessedTax at all.
- `us-gaap:OperatingLeasesIncomeStatementLeaseRevenue` — Rental revenue line · USD · FY2009-2020 · FY2018 2,388,791,000; FY2019 2,831,818,000; FY2020 3,791,131,000 · DEAD after FY2020. From FY2021 the rental line is pld:RentalRevenue (extension; FY2025 8,158,904,000 per R4.htm) and is absent from companyfacts. Value-probed 8,158,904,000 — zero hits.
- `us-gaap:DirectCostsOfLeasedAndRentedPropertyOrEquipment` — Rental operating expense · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023 1,624,793,000; FY2024 1,765,385,000; FY2025 1,964,137,000 · Continuous. Pairs with pld:RentalRevenue to build property NOI by hand, but the revenue half is extension-only from FY2021.
- `us-gaap:StraightLineRent` — Straight-line rent adjustment — legacy · USD · FY2010-2014 · FY2012 62,290,000; FY2013 46,861,000; FY2014 42,829,000 · DEAD after FY2014. From FY2015 the cash-flow add-back is pld:StraightLineRentsAndAmortizationOfAboveAndBelowMarketLeases (extension): FY2023 (613,005,000); FY2024 (644,606,000); FY2025 (691,090,000), read off R8.htm. That is 8.5% of FY2025 rental revenue booked as revenue before the cash arrives, and it is invisible to companyfacts.
- `us-gaap:BelowMarketLeaseNet` — Above/below-market lease intangibles · USD · FY2012-2025 · FY2023 2,206,906,000; FY2024 1,778,832,000; FY2025 1,437,612,000 · Balance, not the amortisation flow. us-gaap:BelowMarketLeaseAccumulatedAmortization FY2022-2025 (FY2025 1,500,000,000, coarse). In-place leases: us-gaap:FiniteLivedIntangibleAssetAcquiredInPlaceLeases FY2015-2025 (FY2025 588,427,000). The forward P&L effect is tagged as extensions pld:NetIncreaseDecreaseToRentalRevenue* — not in companyfacts.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsForLeasingCostsCommissionsAndTenantImprovements` — Tenant improvements and leasing commissions (recurring leasing capex) · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023 388,814,000; FY2024 499,927,000; FY2025 562,197,000 · The single most useful AFFO tag on this desk and it IS in companyfacts, continuously, for PLD. Cash-flow caption: 'Tenant improvements and lease commissions on previously leased space' — explicitly the re-leasing cost, not development.
- `pld:PaymentForPropertyImprovements` — Property improvements (building maintenance capex) · USD · FY2023-2025 · FY2023 303,042,000; FY2024 386,481,000; FY2025 327,355,000 · Extension; NOT in companyfacts (value-probed 327,355,000 and 386,481,000 — zero hits). Its us-gaap predecessor us-gaap:PaymentsForCapitalImprovements ran FY2009-FY2018 only (FY2018 93,073,000). So the roofs-and-lifts line is separable from development in the filing, but only by reading the filing.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsToDevelopRealEstateAssets` — Development spend · USD · FY2017-2025 · FY2023 3,399,114,000; FY2024 3,206,231,000; FY2025 2,781,260,000 · Clean and separable from maintenance. Predecessor us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireAndDevelopRealEstate FY2009-2018 combined acquisition AND development in one number — do not chain the two series.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireRealEstate` — Acquisitions and disposition proceeds · USD · FY2010-2025 · FY2023 4,195,714,000; FY2024 2,327,605,000; FY2025 1,800,527,000 · Proceeds side: us-gaap:ProceedsFromRealEstateAndRealEstateJointVentures FY2013-2025 (FY2023 1,764,322,000; FY2024 3,790,388,000; FY2025 2,246,186,000). The gap between proceeds and gains is the recycling Munger would want isolated.
- `us-gaap:LongTermDebt` — Total debt · USD · FY2011-2025 · FY2023 29,000,501,000; FY2024 30,879,263,000; FY2025 35,037,073,000 · Continuous and complete; PLD carries no separate current-maturities tag to add. Legacy us-gaap:DebtAndCapitalLeaseObligations FY2010-2014 only.
- `us-gaap:LongtermDebtWeightedAverageInterestRate` — Weighted-average interest rate on debt · pure · FY2018-2025 · FY2023 0.030; FY2024 0.031; FY2025 0.032 · PLD is the only one of the three that tags a weighted-average rate at all. Legacy us-gaap:DebtWeightedAverageInterestRate FY2010-2012 (0.044-0.051), then a six-year hole FY2013-2017.
- `us-gaap:LongTermDebtMaturitiesRepaymentsOfPrincipalInNextTwelveMonths` — Debt maturities · USD · FY2011-2025 · FY2023 530,519,000; FY2024 514,223,000; FY2025 1,914,530,000 · Year-one is continuous FY2011-2025. The after-year-five companion us-gaap:LongTermDebtMaturitiesRepaymentsOfPrincipalAfterYearFive exists FY2011, FY2012 then jumps to FY2017-2025 (FY2025 22,554,789,000) — a five-year hole in the middle of the ladder.
- `us-gaap:InterestCostsCapitalizedAdjustment` — Capitalised interest · USD · FY2013-2025 · FY2023 116,620,000; FY2024 107,565,000; FY2025 107,108,000 · Predecessor us-gaap:InterestCostsCapitalized FY2009-2014 overlaps FY2013-2014 and agrees, so the seam is checkable. Note the element name says 'Adjustment' — it is the amount capitalised into development, i.e. interest that never hits the income statement.
- `us-gaap:PreferredStockValue` — Preferred equity · USD · FY2010-2025 · FY2023 63,948,000; FY2024 63,948,000; FY2025 63,948,000 · Trivial and static. Preferred dividend: us-gaap:PreferredStockDividendsIncomeStatementImpact FY2013-2025 (FY2025 5,882,000). us-gaap:PreferredStockLiquidationPreference 50 USD/share.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsOfDividends` — Dividends and distributions paid (cash) · USD · FY2012-2025 · FY2023 3,228,589,000; FY2024 3,570,480,000; FY2025 3,764,745,000 · The cash-flow caption is 'Dividends/distributions paid on common and preferred stock/units' — it INCLUDES preferred dividends and OP-unit distributions, so it is not a clean common-dividend numerator. us-gaap:PaymentsOfDividendsCommonStock died after FY2013.
- `us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid` — Dividends per share · USD/shares · FY2009-2025 · FY2023 3.48; FY2024 3.84; FY2025 4.04 · This is the usable per-share series. The obvious sibling us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared is present FY2009-2025 with the SAME annual duration contexts but values of 0.00 (FY2023), 0.01 (FY2024), 0.03 (FY2025) — see hazards.
- ABSENT: FFO — no tag, us-gaap or extension.; AFFO / Core FFO / Cash available for distribution — no tag.; Same-store or same-property NOI — no tag, us-gaap or extension. Not even a NetOperatingIncome extension element exists in pld-20251231.xsd.; Impairment — us-gaap:ImpairmentOfRealEstate ends FY2012; no annual impairment tag from FY2013 forward.; Rental revenue as a us-gaap tag from FY2021 forward.; Straight-line rent as a us-gaap tag from FY2015 forward.; Maintenance/property-improvement capex as a us-gaap tag from FY2019 forward.; Gains on property sales as a us-gaap tag from FY2019 forward.; us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquirePropertyPlantAndEquipment — absent entirely.
- HAZARDS: FFO IS NOT TAGGED. There is no FundsFromOperations/AFFO element in PLD's companyfacts and none in the FY2025 extension schema pld-20251231.xsd (I grepped that schema for Funds, FFO, AdjustedFunds, NetOperatingIncome, SameStore, SameProperty, Maintenance, Recurring — every hit was a false positive on an unrelated element name). FFO is disclosed only in the earnings release, which is not XBRL-tagged. The desk cannot extract PLD's FFO; it can only build one.; THE REPO'S FFO COLUMN PRODUCES A WRONG NUMBER FOR PLD. src/lib/reits.mjs ffo() = netIncome + depreciation − (gainOnSaleRealEstate || 0), and scripts/fetchFundamentals.mjs maps gainOnSaleRealEstate to [GainLossOnSaleOfPropertiesNetOfApplicableIncomeTaxes, GainLossOnDispositionOfRealEstate, GainsLossesOnSalesOfInvestmentRealEstate, GainLossOnSaleOfProperties, GainLossOnDispositionOfAssets1]. None of those five has an annual PLD frame after FY2018, so the `|| 0` fires. Arithmetic on verified filed inputs: printed FFO = 3,328,231,000 + 2,626,028,000 − 0 = 5,954,259,000. A NAREIT-shaped figure using real-estate depreciation and the actual gains = 3,328,231,000 + 2,011,907,000 − 943,562,000 = 4,396,576,000. The printed figure is 35% high, and the dividend-coverage cell flips from 63% (3,764,745,000 ÷ 5,954,259) to 86% (÷ 4,396,576) — from 'Lightly covered' to 'Covered', on the wrong side of the only question that matters.; AFTER MAINTENANCE, PLD'S FY2025 DISTRIBUTION IS NOT EARNED ON THESE INPUTS. Tenant improvements and leasing commissions 562,197,000 plus property improvements 327,355,000 = 889,552,000. 4,396,576,000 − 889,552,000 = 3,507,024,000 against distributions paid of 3,764,745,000. Arithmetic on verified filed lines, not a NAREIT-defined AFFO; but it is the shape of the answer the desk exists to give, and the current column gives the opposite one.; us-gaap:CommonStockDividendsPerShareDeclared is a TRAP for PLD. Annual duration facts (start 2025-01-01, end 2025-12-31, accn 0001193125-26-051453) carry 0.03; FY2024 0.01; FY2023 0.00; FY2022 0.02. The actual rate is 4.04. Whatever PLD is tagging there, it is not the annual dividend. Use CommonStockDividendsPerShareCashPaid.; us-gaap:OperatingIncomeLoss for PLD sits BELOW the two gains-on-disposition lines and includes them (FY2025 4,357,864,000 vs 3,414,302,000 before gains). Any operating margin, EBITDA or interest-coverage figure built on it for PLD is inflated by asset sales.; us-gaap:DepreciationDepletionAndAmortization (FY2009-2015) and us-gaap:DepreciationAndAmortization (FY2009-2025) coexist in the same years with materially different values (FY2014: 642,461,000 vs 1,672m-class). A concept list that prefers the first tag with a value silently switches definitions at FY2015.; Prologis, Inc. and Prologis, L.P. (CIK 0001045610) file a SINGLE combined 10-K under one accession — 0001193125-26-051453 appears as a 10-K in BOTH entities' submissions feeds. The LP's companyfacts is a near-empty shell containing only `ffd` filing-fee facts from 424B2 prospectuses: no financial statement facts whatsoever. So there is no double-count risk from companyfacts, but any router that resolves the LP CIK gets a dead record, and the combined instance uses srt:ConsolidatedEntitiesAxis to separate Inc from LP columns (dimensioned facts, invisible to companyfacts either way).; us-gaap:NetCashProvidedByUsedInOperatingActivities has a hole at FY2015 (ends run 2009-2014 then 2016-2025).

### AMT (CIK 0001053507, FYE 12)

- `us-gaap:NetIncomeLoss` — Net income attributable to parent — ABANDONED TAG · USD · FY2007-2020 · FY2018 1,236,400,000; FY2019 1,887,800,000; FY2020 1,690,600,000 · AMT stopped using this element. Across the whole companyfacts file the maximum end date on any NetIncomeLoss fact of any form is 2022-09-30, and the last annual 10-K frame is FY2020. A concept list that leads with NetIncomeLoss returns nothing for AMT FY2021-FY2025.
- `us-gaap:NetIncomeLossAvailableToCommonStockholdersBasic` — Net income to common — the usable series · USD · FY2012-2025 · FY2023 1,483,300,000; FY2024 2,255,000,000; FY2025 2,529,500,000 · Continuous through FY2025. Note FY2023 available-to-common (1,483,300,000) EXCEEDS ProfitLoss (1,367,100,000) because noncontrolling interests absorbed losses — a naive sanity check of 'available-to-common must be <= total' will fire falsely.
- `us-gaap:ProfitLoss` — Net income including all NCI · USD · FY2007-2025 · FY2023 1,367,100,000; FY2024 2,280,200,000; FY2025 2,628,500,000 · Continuous. AMT also files us-gaap:NetIncomeLossIncludingPortionAttributableToNonredeemableNoncontrollingInterest (facts through 2026-03-31) because it has both redeemable and non-redeemable NCI — a four-deep net-income ladder.
- `us-gaap:DepreciationAmortizationAndAccretionNet` — Total D&A and accretion (cash-flow add-back) · USD · FY2007-2025 · FY2023 2,928,500,000; FY2024 2,028,800,000; FY2025 2,041,600,000 · RESTATED: FY2023 was filed at 3,086,500,000 in accn 0001053507-24-000011 (2024-02-27) and re-presented at 2,928,500,000 in accn 0001053507-25-000025 (2025-02-25) after ATC India went to discontinued operations. Same fiscal year, two filed values. Also note the FY2023-to-FY2024 drop of 900m is the India disposal, not an economic change in the tower base.
- `us-gaap:SECScheduleIIIRealEstateAccumulatedDepreciationDepreciationExpense` — Real-estate-only depreciation (Schedule III) · USD · FY2012-2025 · FY2023 1,353,500,000; FY2024 1,002,300,000; FY2025 974,000,000 · The tag to use for a real-estate-specific FFO add-back. It is roughly half of the total D&A tag — the rest is intangible amortisation on acquired customer relationships, which NAREIT FFO does NOT add back.
- `us-gaap:Depreciation` — Depreciation (disclosure note, coarse) · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023 1,400,000,000; FY2024 1,100,000,000; FY2025 1,100,000,000 · Rounded to the nearest 100,000,000 — a narrative-note figure, not a statement line. Do not build a ratio on it.
- `us-gaap:AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets` — Intangible amortisation · USD · FY2010-2025 · FY2023 1,400,000,000; FY2024 892,000,000; FY2025 879,700,000 · FY2023 is the coarse 1,400,000,000; FY2024/25 are exact to 100,000. Precision changes inside the same series.
- `us-gaap:GainLossOnSaleOfOtherAssets` — Gains/losses on asset sales — the only surviving tag · USD · FY2009-2025 · FY2023 -131,300,000; FY2024 -17,900,000; FY2025 17,300,000 · Continuous FY2009-2025 but NOT in the repo's gainOnSaleRealEstate list. Sign convention unverified against the income-statement presentation (AMT reports 'Other operating expenses' as a positive expense), so do not subtract it blind. Legacy us-gaap:GainLossOnSalesOfAssetsAndAssetImpairmentCharges ran FY2010-2017 and mixed gains with impairments in one number.
- `us-gaap:AssetImpairmentCharges` — Impairments · USD · FY2008-2025 · FY2023 280,000,000; FY2024 68,600,000; FY2025 100,700,000 · Companion us-gaap:ImpairmentOfLongLivedAssetsHeldForUse FY2023 202,400,000 / FY2024 68,600,000 / FY2025 94,200,000 — overlaps and partially duplicates. us-gaap:GoodwillImpairmentLoss is sparse (FY2009, 2010, 2014, 2020-2024; FY2023 80,000,000). us-gaap:RestructuringCostsAndAssetImpairmentCharges FY2007-2025 is a wider bucket (FY2023 739,900,000).
- `us-gaap:Revenues` — Total revenue · USD · FY2016-2025 · FY2023 10,012,200,000; FY2024 10,127,200,000; FY2025 10,644,600,000 · THIS is total revenue. See hazards — the ASC 606 tag is a small slice, not the total.

- `us-gaap:LongTermDebtMaturitiesRepaymentsOfPrincipalInNextTwelveMonths` — Debt maturities — DEAD · USD · FY2010-2022 · FY2020 789,800,000; FY2021 4,568,700,000; FY2022 4,514,200,000 · AMT stopped tagging the maturity ladder undimensioned after FY2022; the same is true of ...AfterYearFive (last FY2022, 14,541,900,000). For the last three years, when the debt comes due is not extractable from companyfacts.
- `us-gaap:PaymentsOfDividendsCommonStock` — Common dividends paid · USD · FY2010-2025 · FY2023 3,006,700,000; FY2024 3,027,300,000; FY2025 3,180,800,000 · The FY2025 10-K cash-flow statement line 'Distributions paid on common stock' is 3,157.2m and is tagged with the extension amt:PaymentsOfOrdinaryDividendsCommonStockAndShareBasedPaymentArrangement.
